<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Collapsible log of what an agent did in a turn ("Worked for</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * 25s" row): a quiet header with the elapsed time, opening to the step list.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> WorkSummary</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
